================================================================================
PATH: ./db_schema.sql
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- WARNING: This schema is for context only and is not meant to be run.
-- Table order and constraints may not be valid for execution.

CREATE TABLE public.announcements (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  title text NOT NULL,
  message text NOT NULL,
  type text DEFAULT 'info'::text CHECK (type = ANY (ARRAY['info'::text, 'warning'::text, 'success'::text])),
  active boolean DEFAULT true,
  CONSTRAINT announcements_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.contact_messages (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  name text,
  email text,
  message text,
  CONSTRAINT contact_messages_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.lesson_progress (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  lesson_id uuid NOT NULL,
  course_id text NOT NULL,
  CONSTRAINT lesson_progress_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT lesson_progress_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  CONSTRAINT lesson_progress_lesson_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (lesson_id) REFERENCES public.lessons(id),
  CONSTRAINT lesson_progress_course_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (course_id) REFERENCES public.products(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.lessons (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  course_id text NOT NULL,
  title text NOT NULL,
  description text,
  video_url text,
  pdf_url text,
  quizlet_url text,
  type text DEFAULT 'video'::text,
  quiz_data jsonb,
  CONSTRAINT lessons_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT lessons_course_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (course_id) REFERENCES public.products(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.products (
  id text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  title text NOT NULL,
  description text,
  price text NOT NULL,
  type text NOT NULL,
  image_url text,
  event_date timestamp with time zone,
  CONSTRAINT products_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.profiles (
  id uuid NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamp with time zone,
  full_name text,
  avatar_url text,
  role text DEFAULT 'student'::text,
  CONSTRAINT profiles_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT profiles_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (id) REFERENCES auth.users(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.purchases (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  product_id text NOT NULL,
  status text DEFAULT 'completed'::text,
  CONSTRAINT purchases_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT purchases_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  CONSTRAINT purchases_product_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES public.products(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.reviews (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  product_id text NOT NULL,
  rating integer NOT NULL CHECK (rating >= 1 AND rating <= 5),
  comment text,
  CONSTRAINT reviews_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT reviews_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  CONSTRAINT reviews_product_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES public.products(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.user_notes (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  lesson_id uuid NOT NULL,
  content text,
  CONSTRAINT user_notes_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id),
  CONSTRAINT user_notes_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id),
  CONSTRAINT user_notes_lesson_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (lesson_id) REFERENCES public.lessons(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.user_stats (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  xp integer DEFAULT 0,
  streak integer DEFAULT 0,
  last_activity date DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
  CONSTRAINT user_stats_pkey PRIMARY KEY (user_id),
  CONSTRAINT user_stats_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES public.profiles(id)
);
CREATE TABLE public.webinar_leads (
  id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  created_at timestamp with time zone NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  full_name text NOT NULL,
  email text NOT NULL,
  webinar_date text,
  gdpr_consent boolean DEFAULT false,
  CONSTRAINT webinar_leads_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

import js from '@eslint/js'
import globals from 'globals'
import reactHooks from 'eslint-plugin-react-hooks'
import reactRefresh from 'eslint-plugin-react-refresh'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}'],
    extends: [
      js.configs.recommended,
      reactHooks.configs.flat.recommended,
      reactRefresh.configs.vite,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      ecmaVersion: 2020,
      globals: globals.browser,
      parserOptions: {
        ecmaVersion: 'latest',
        ecmaFeatures: { jsx: true },
        sourceType: 'module',
      },
    },
    rules: {
      'no-unused-vars': ['error', { varsIgnorePattern: '^[A-Z_]' }],
    },
  },

    pkgs.nodejs_20

  # Configurazione specifica per l'editor IDX

    # Estensioni di VS Code da installare (opzionale)
    extensions = [
      # "svelte.svelte-vscode"
      # "vue.volar"
    ];

    # Configurazione Anteprima
    previews = {
      enable = true;
      previews = {
        web = {
          # Comando per avviare Next.js
          command = [
            "npm"
            "run"
            "dev"
            "--"
            "--port"
            "$PORT"
            "--hostname"
            "0.0.0.0"
          ];
          manager = "web";
          env = {
            # Variabili d'ambiente opzionali per l'anteprima
            PORT = "$PORT";
          };
        };
      };
    };
  };
}

      { protocol: 'https', hostname: 'images.unsplash.com' },
      { protocol: 'https', hostname: 'lkqbkhaklhenhlahgvhj.supabase.co' }
    ],
  },
  // Handle SPA-style fallbacks if necessary (though App Router handles this natively)
  async rewrites() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/:path*',
        destination: '/',
      },
    ]
  },
}

# 🇰🇷 Annyeong Akadémia - Web Platform & LMS

## 📋 Project Background & Context
**Annyeong Akadémia** is a Korean language school tailored for the Hungarian market. The core value proposition is teaching Korean **without requiring English knowledge**, using a direct Korean-to-Hungarian method.

**⚠️ Development Context:**
This project was developed as an **emergency pivot** to meet the strict deadline of **December 6th** for the first live webinar. Due to previous delays with other solutions, this platform was built from scratch using a **Modern Tech Stack (React + Supabase)** to ensure speed, stability, and full control over specific custom features that standard CMS plugins could not easily handle.

---

## 🎯 Part 1: Webinar Landing Page (Priority: Immediate)
**Objective:** Capture sign-ups for the "Korean Open Day" free webinar.

### ✅ Business Specifications Implemented
*   **Event Dates:** Users can select between two specific appointments:
    *   📅 **December 6th (Friday)**
    *   📅 **December 7th (Saturday)**
*   **GDPR Compliant Registration:**
    *   Replaced Google Forms with a custom, professional form.
    *   **Data Collected:** Name, Email, Date Preference.
    *   **Consent:** Mandatory checkbox to accept Privacy Policy before submission.
*   **Email Automation Ready:** The system records leads into the `webinar_leads` database table, which can trigger webhook automations (e.g., via Resend or MailerLite) to send the Zoom link confirmation.
*   **Content Anatomy (EduBlink Style):**
    *   **Hero:** "Learn Korean without English" USP with social proof (+500 students).
    *   **Benefits:** Bento-grid layout highlighting Native Teachers and Cultural Experience.
    *   **Teachers:** Profiles for Kim Seon-saeng-nim (Beginner Specialist) and Lee Seon-saeng-nim.
    *   **Agenda:** Timeline (18:00 Intro -> 18:15 Mini-Lesson -> 18:45 Q&A).
    *   **Offer:** "0 Ft" vs "15.000 Ft" value comparison.
    *   **Footer:** Links to Privacy Policy, Terms, and Contact.

---

## 🎯 Part 2: Main LMS Platform (Deadline: Dec 6)
**Objective:** Enable course enrollment, content delivery, and digital product sales immediately following the webinar.

### ✅ Core Functions Implemented

#### 1. Course Management
*   **Regular Group Courses:**
    *   Support for fixed-schedule, curriculum-based courses (e.g., "Coreano Base 1").
    *   **Membership System:** Content is locked. Only users who purchase the course can access the Player.
*   **Standalone Live Lessons (Drop-in):**
    *   Booking system for single sessions (e.g., "Learn with K-Pop").
    *   **Logic:** These do not build on each other; schedules vary weekly.
    *   **Delivery:** Upon purchase, the "Buy" button transforms into a **"Join Zoom"** button.

#### 2. Digital Store (E-Commerce)
*   **Products:** Dedicated section for purchasing **Ebooks** and **Planners**.
*   **Delivery:** "My Downloads" area where students can download PDFs immediately after purchase.

#### 3. Content Delivery (The Player)
*   **Video/Audio:** Supports streaming of pre-recorded lessons.
*   **File Support:** Teachers can upload **PDFs**, **Audio (MP3)**, and **PPT (Presentations)** directly to specific lessons.
*   **Quizlet Integration:** Specific functionality to add external Quizlet Flashcard buttons to any lesson.

#### 4. Institutional Pages
*   **About Us:** Mission statement and team overview.
*   **Our Tutors:** Detailed profiles.
*   **Contact Us:** Functional form saving messages to the database (`contact_messages`).
*   **Legal:** GDPR and Terms & Conditions pages.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

*   **Frontend:** React 18 (Vite)
*   **Styling:** Tailwind CSS (Custom "Brand Purple/Blue" gradient theme)
*   **Routing:** React Router DOM v6 (Protected Routes for Dashboard/Admin)
*   **Backend-as-a-Service:** Supabase
    *   **Database:** PostgreSQL.
    *   **Auth:** Secure Email/Password authentication.
    *   **Storage:** Hosting for Course PDFs, Audio files, and PPTs.
*   **Payments:** Logic ready for Stripe Integration (currently in Simulation Mode).
---

## 🚀 Setup & Installation

### 1. Database Configuration
This project uses **Supabase**.
1. Create a new Supabase project.
2. Go to the SQL Editor in your dashboard.
3. Run the contents of these files in order:
   - `final_db_setup.sql` (Creates Tables & Security Policies)
   - `storage_setup.sql` (Enables File Uploads)
   - `seed_data.sql` (Adds Demo Courses & Products)

### 2. Environment Variables
1. Rename `.env.local.example` (if it exists) to `.env.local`.
2. Fill in your keys from Supabase -> Project Settings -> API:
    ```
    VITE_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
    VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
    ```

### 3. Run Locally
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

### 4. Admin Access
To access the Admin Dashboard at `/admin`:
1. Sign up as a new user in the app.
2. Go to Supabase Dashboard -> Table Editor -> `profiles`.
3. Find your user row and change the `role` column from `student` to `admin`.
4. Refresh the page.

PATH: ./src/app/(app)/admin/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { 
  Upload, Package, LayoutList, Loader2, Trash2, RefreshCw, Users, 
  Image as ImageIcon, Edit, X, FileText, Video, Mic, GraduationCap, 
  PlusCircle, DollarSign, Megaphone, Calendar, HelpCircle, 
  Mail, AlertTriangle, Info 
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { extractTag, cleanDescription, generateFilePath } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const { isAdmin, loading: authLoading } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()
  
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('lessons')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null)
  
  // UI State
  const [showQuizHelp, setShowQuizHelp] = useState(false)
  
  // Data
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [allLessons, setAllLessons] = useState([])
  const [allProducts, setAllProducts] = useState([])
  const [leads, setLeads] = useState([])
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([])
  const [purchases, setPurchases] = useState([])
  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState([])

  // Forms
  const [lessonData, setLessonData] = useState({ 
    title: "", course_id: "", video_url: "", quizlet_url: "", description: "", type: "video", pdf_url: "",
    quiz_data: "" 
  })
  const [lessonMediaFile, setLessonMediaFile] = useState(null)
  const [lessonPdfFile, setLessonPdfFile] = useState(null)
  
  const [productData, setProductData] = useState({ id: "", title: "", price: "", type: "course", description: "", image_url: "", zoom_link: "", download_link: "", event_date: "" })
  const [productCoverFile, setProductCoverFile] = useState(null)
  const [productEbookFile, setProductEbookFile] = useState(null)

  const [announcementData, setAnnouncementData] = useState({ title: "", message: "", type: "info", active: true })
  const [selectedCourseToEnroll, setSelectedCourseToEnroll] = useState("")

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authLoading && !isAdmin) router.push('/dashboard')
    fetchData()
  }, [isAdmin, authLoading, navigate])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const { data: prodData } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      if (prodData) {
        setAllProducts(prodData)
        const courseList = prodData.filter(p => p.type === 'course')
        setCourses(courseList)
        if(courseList.length > 0 && !lessonData.course_id) setLessonData(prev => ({ ...prev, course_id: courseList[0].id }))
      }
      const { data: lessData } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('*, products(title)').order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      if (lessData) setAllLessons(lessData)
      const { data: leadsData } = await supabase.from('webinar_leads').select('*').order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      if (leadsData) setLeads(leadsData)
      const { data: msgData } = await supabase.from('contact_messages').select('*').order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      if (msgData) setMessages(msgData)
      
      // FIX: Sorted by updated_at because profiles doesn't have created_at
      const { data: usersData } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').order('updated_at', {ascending: false})
      if (usersData) setUsers(usersData)
      
      const { data: purchaseData } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('user_id, product_id')
      if (purchaseData) setPurchases(purchaseData)
      const { data: annData } = await supabase.from('announcements').select('*').order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      if (annData) setAnnouncements(annData)
    } catch(e) { console.error(e) }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const uploadToStorage = async (file, bucket, folder) => {
    if (!file) return null
    const path = generateFilePath(folder, file)
    const { error } = await supabase.storage.from(bucket).upload(path, file)
    if (error) throw error
    const { data } = supabase.storage.from(bucket).getPublicUrl(path)
    return data.publicUrl
  }

  const handleManualEnroll = async (userId) => {
    if (!selectedCourseToEnroll) return alert("Select a course first")
    if (!window.confirm("Enroll user for free?")) return
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{ user_id: userId, product_id: selectedCourseToEnroll, status: 'completed' }])
    setLoading(false)
    if (error) alert(error.message)
    else { alert("Enrolled!"); fetchData(); }
  }

  const handleSaveAnnouncement = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.from('announcements').insert([announcementData])
    setLoading(false)
    if (error) alert(error.message)
    else { alert("Announcement posted!"); setAnnouncementData({ title: "", message: "", type: "info", active: true }); fetchData(); }
  }

  const startEditLesson = (l) => {
    setEditingId(l.id)
    setLessonData({
      title: l.title, course_id: l.course_id, type: l.type || 'video',
      video_url: l.video_url || "", pdf_url: l.pdf_url || "", quizlet_url: l.quizlet_url || "", description: l.description || "",
      quiz_data: l.quiz_data ? JSON.stringify(l.quiz_data, null, 2) : ""
    })
    window.scrollTo(0,0)
  }

  const startEditProduct = (p) => {
    setEditingId(p.id)
    setProductData({
      id: p.id, title: p.title, price: p.price, type: p.type,
      description: cleanDescription(p.description),
      image_url: p.image_url || "",
      zoom_link: extractTag(p.description, 'ZOOM') || "",
      download_link: extractTag(p.description, 'DOWNLOAD_URL') || "",
      event_date: p.event_date ? new Date(p.event_date).toISOString().slice(0, 16) : ""
    })
    window.scrollTo(0,0)
  }

  const cancelEdit = () => {
    setEditingId(null)
    setProductData({ id: "", title: "", price: "", type: "course", description: "", image_url: "", zoom_link: "", download_link: "", event_date: "" })
    setLessonData({ ...lessonData, title: "", video_url: "", pdf_url: "", quizlet_url: "", description: "", quiz_data: "" })
    setLessonMediaFile(null); setLessonPdfFile(null); setProductCoverFile(null); setProductEbookFile(null)
  }

  const handleSaveProduct = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      let finalImage = productData.image_url
      if (productCoverFile) finalImage = await uploadToStorage(productCoverFile, 'course-materials', 'covers')
      let finalDownload = productData.download_link
      if (productEbookFile) finalDownload = await uploadToStorage(productEbookFile, 'course-materials', 'ebooks')
      let finalDesc = productData.description
      if (productData.type === 'standalone' && productData.zoom_link) finalDesc += `\n\n[[ZOOM::${productData.zoom_link}]]`
      if (productData.type === 'ebook' && finalDownload) finalDesc += `\n\n[[DOWNLOAD_URL::${finalDownload}]]`
      
      const payload = { 
        title: productData.title, price: productData.price, type: productData.type, 
        description: finalDesc, image_url: finalImage,
        event_date: productData.event_date || null 
      }
      const pid = productData.id || productData.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '-')
      if (editingId) await supabase.from('products').update(payload).eq('id', editingId)
      else await supabase.from('products').insert([{ ...payload, id: pid }])
      alert("Product saved!"); cancelEdit(); fetchData()
    } catch (err) { alert(err.message) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handleSaveLesson = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      let finalVideo = lessonData.video_url
      if (lessonData.type === 'audio' && lessonMediaFile) finalVideo = await uploadToStorage(lessonMediaFile, 'course-materials', 'lessons/audio')
      let finalPdf = lessonData.pdf_url
      if (lessonPdfFile) finalPdf = await uploadToStorage(lessonPdfFile, 'course-materials', 'lessons/pdf')
      
      let parsedQuiz = null
      if (lessonData.quiz_data.trim()) {
        try { parsedQuiz = JSON.parse(lessonData.quiz_data) } catch { throw new Error("Invalid JSON for Quiz Data") }
      }

      const payload = { 
        course_id: lessonData.course_id, title: lessonData.title, type: lessonData.type, 
        video_url: finalVideo, pdf_url: finalPdf, quizlet_url: lessonData.quizlet_url, 
        description: lessonData.description, quiz_data: parsedQuiz
      }
      
      if (editingId) await supabase.from('lessons').update(payload).eq('id', editingId)
      else await supabase.from('lessons').insert([payload])
      alert("Lesson saved!"); cancelEdit(); fetchData()
    } catch (err) { alert(err.message) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handleDelete = async (table, id) => {
    if(!window.confirm("Delete?")) return;
    await supabase.from(table).delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchData()
  }

  // Helper to separate Contacts from Reports
  const contactsList = messages.filter(m => m.email !== 'system@report.com')
  const reportsList = messages.filter(m => m.email === 'system@report.com')

  if (authLoading || !isAdmin) return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Admin Dashboard</h1>
        <button onClick={fetchData} className="p-2 bg-white shadow-sm rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 text-brand-purple"><RefreshCw size={20}/></button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100"><div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-500 mb-1"><GraduationCap size={18}/><span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">Students</span></div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{users.length}</p></div>
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100"><div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-500 mb-1"><DollarSign size={18}/><span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">Purchases</span></div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{purchases.length}</p></div>
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100"><div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-500 mb-1"><Users size={18}/><span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">Leads</span></div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{leads.length}</p></div>
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100"><div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-500 mb-1"><AlertTriangle size={18}/><span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase">Reports</span></div><p className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{reportsList.length}</p></div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-8 border-b border-gray-200 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {[
          { id: 'lessons', icon: LayoutList, label: 'Lessons' },
          { id: 'products', icon: Package, label: 'Products' },
          { id: 'students', icon: GraduationCap, label: 'Students' },
          { id: 'announce', icon: Megaphone, label: 'Announce' },
          { id: 'leads', icon: Users, label: 'Leads' },
          { id: 'contacts', icon: Mail, label: 'Contacts' },
          { id: 'reports', icon: AlertTriangle, label: 'Reports' }
        ].map(tab => (
          <button key={tab.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 font-bold rounded-t-lg transition whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}>
            <tab.icon size={18}/> {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* TAB CONTENT */}
      {activeTab === 'announce' && (
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
           <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-lg border-t-4 border-orange-500 h-fit sticky top-24">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800 mb-6">New Announcement</h2>
              <form onSubmit={handleSaveAnnouncement} className="space-y-4">
                <input required type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="Title" value={announcementData.title} onChange={e => setAnnouncementData({...announcementData, title: e.target.value})} />
                <textarea required className="input-field" placeholder="Message..." rows="3" value={announcementData.message} onChange={e => setAnnouncementData({...announcementData, message: e.target.value})} />
                <select className="input-field" value={announcementData.type} onChange={e => setAnnouncementData({...announcementData, type: e.target.value})}><option value="info">Info</option><option value="warning">Warning</option><option value="success">Success</option></select>
                <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="btn-primary w-full bg-orange-500 hover:bg-orange-600">{loading ? "..." : "Post"}</button>
              </form>
           </div>
           <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-3">{announcements.map(ann => (<div key={ann.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center"><div><h4 className="font-bold">{ann.title}</h4><p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{ann.message}</p></div><button onClick={() => handleDelete('announcements', ann.id)} className="text-red-500"><Trash2 size={18}/></button></div>))}</div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeTab === 'students' && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-6 overflow-x-auto">
             <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4"><select className="input-field" value={selectedCourseToEnroll} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourseToEnroll(e.target.value)}><option value="">Select Course...</option>{courses.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}</option>)}</select></div>
             <table className="w-full text-sm text-left"><thead className="text-gray-400 font-bold bg-gray-50"><tr><th className="p-4">User</th><th className="p-4">Role</th><th className="p-4 text-right">Action</th></tr></thead>
             <tbody>{users.map(u => (<tr key={u.id} className="border-t"><td className="p-4"><p className="font-bold">{u.full_name}</p><p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{u.email}</p></td><td className="p-4">{u.role}</td><td className="p-4 text-right"><button onClick={() => handleManualEnroll(u.id)} className="text-green-600 bg-green-50 px-3 py-1 rounded font-bold text-xs">Enroll</button></td></tr>))}</tbody></table>
          </div>
      )}

      {activeTab === 'lessons' && (
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
           <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-lg border-t-4 border-brand-purple h-fit sticky top-24">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800 mb-6">{editingId ? 'Edit' : 'New'} Lesson</h2>
              <form onSubmit={handleSaveLesson} className="space-y-4">
                <select className="input-field" value={lessonData.course_id} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, course_id: e.target.value})}>{courses.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}</option>)}</select>
                <input required type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="Title" value={lessonData.title} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, title: e.target.value})} />
                <select className="input-field" value={lessonData.type} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, type: e.target.value})}><option value="video">Video</option><option value="audio">Audio</option></select>
                {lessonData.type === 'video' ? <input type="url" className="input-field" placeholder="Video URL" value={lessonData.video_url} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, video_url: e.target.value})} /> : <input type="file" className="input-field" onChange={e => setLessonMediaFile(e.target.files[0])} />}
                <input type="file" className="input-field" onChange={e => setLessonPdfFile(e.target.files[0])} />
                <textarea className="input-field" placeholder="Desc" rows="3" value={lessonData.description} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, description: e.target.value})} />
                
                {/* QUIZ EDITOR WITH HELP */}
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                   <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                     <label className="label flex items-center gap-1 text-brand-purple mb-0">Quiz Data (JSON)</label>
                     <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowQuizHelp(!showQuizHelp)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple transition p-1 rounded-full hover:bg-purple-100">
                       <HelpCircle size={16} />
                     </button>
                   </div>
                   
                   {showQuizHelp && (
                     <div className="mb-3 p-3 bg-white rounded border border-purple-100 text-xs text-gray-500 font-mono shadow-sm animate-slide-in">
                       <p className="font-bold mb-2 text-brand-purple">Format Example:</p>
                       <pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap">
{`[
  {
    "question": "Is this correct?",
    "options": ["Yes", "No"],
    "correct": 0
  },
  {
    "question": "Is this wrong?",
    "options": ["Yes", "No"],
    "correct": 1
  }
]`}
                       </pre>
                     </div>
                   )}

                   <textarea className="input-field text-xs font-mono" placeholder='[{"question":"...","options":["..."],"correct":0}]' rows="3" value={lessonData.quiz_data} onChange={e => setLessonData({...lessonData, quiz_data: e.target.value})} />
                </div>

                <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="btn-primary w-full">{loading ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</button>
                {editingId && <button type="button" onClick={cancelEdit} className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Cancel</button>}
              </form>
           </div>
           <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-3">{allLessons.map(l => (<div key={l.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center"><div><h4 className="font-bold">{l.title}</h4><p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{l.products?.title} {l.quiz_data && '• Quiz Active'}</p></div><div className="flex gap-2"><button onClick={() => startEditLesson(l)} className="text-blue-500"><Edit size={18}/></button><button onClick={() => handleDelete('lessons', l.id)} className="text-red-500"><Trash2 size={18}/></button></div></div>))}</div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeTab === 'products' && (
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
           <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-lg border-t-4 border-brand-blue h-fit sticky top-24">
              <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800 mb-6">{editingId ? 'Edit' : 'New'} Product</h2>
              <form onSubmit={handleSaveProduct} className="space-y-4">
                <input required type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="Title" value={productData.title} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, title: e.target.value})} />
                <input required type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="Price" value={productData.price} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, price: e.target.value})} />
                <select className="input-field" value={productData.type} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, type: e.target.value})}><option value="course">Course</option><option value="ebook">Ebook</option><option value="standalone">Live</option></select>
                {!editingId && <input type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="ID" value={productData.id} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, id: e.target.value})} />}
                
                {productData.type === 'standalone' && (
                   <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-blue-100">
                      <label className="label text-brand-blue flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar size={14}/> Event Date</label>
                      <input type="datetime-local" className="input-field bg-white" value={productData.event_date} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, event_date: e.target.value})} />
                      <input type="url" className="input-field bg-white mt-2" placeholder="Zoom Link" value={productData.zoom_link} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, zoom_link: e.target.value})} />
                   </div>
                )}

                {/* FIXED COVER IMG INPUT */}
                <div className="file-input-wrapper">
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-sm text-gray-600 w-full">
                    <ImageIcon size={16}/> 
                    <span>{productCoverFile ? productCoverFile.name : "Cover Img (.jpg/.png)"}</span>
                    <input type="file" className="hidden" accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/webp" onChange={e => setProductCoverFile(e.target.files[0])} />
                  </label>
                </div>

                {/* ADDED EBOOK INPUT */}
                {productData.type === 'ebook' && (
                  <div className="file-input-wrapper bg-purple-50 border-purple-100">
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-sm text-brand-purple w-full font-bold">
                      <FileText size={16}/> 
                      <span>{productEbookFile ? productEbookFile.name : "Upload PDF (Ebook)"}</span>
                      <input type="file" className="hidden" accept="application/pdf" onChange={e => setProductEbookFile(e.target.files[0])} />
                    </label>
                  </div>
                )}

                <textarea className="input-field" placeholder="Desc" rows="4" value={productData.description} onChange={e => setProductData({...productData, description: e.target.value})} />
                <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="btn-primary w-full bg-brand-blue">{loading ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</button>
                {editingId && <button type="button" onClick={cancelEdit} className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Cancel</button>}
              </form>
           </div>
           <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-3">{allProducts.map(p => (
              <div key={p.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center">
                 <div>
                    <h4 className="font-bold">{p.title}</h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{p.type} • {p.price}</p>
                    {p.event_date && <span className="text-xs bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold mt-1 inline-block">{new Date(p.event_date).toLocaleString()}</span>}
                 </div>
                 <div className="flex gap-2"><button onClick={() => startEditProduct(p)} className="text-blue-500"><Edit size={18}/></button><button onClick={() => handleDelete('products', p.id)} className="text-red-500"><Trash2 size={18}/></button></div>
              </div>
           ))}</div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeTab === 'leads' && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
          {leads.map(l => (
            <div key={l.id} className="border-b py-3 last:border-0 flex justify-between items-center">
              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{l.full_name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{l.email} • {l.webinar_date}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs bg-green-50 text-green-600 px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold">Lead</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CONTACTS TAB */}
      {activeTab === 'contacts' && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Mail size={20}/> Contact Messages</h3>
          {contactsList.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400">No messages.</p>}
          {contactsList.map(m => (
            <div key={m.id} className="border-b border-gray-100 py-4 last:border-0 hover:bg-gray-50 px-2 -mx-2 rounded transition">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{m.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{m.email} • {new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => handleDelete('contact_messages', m.id)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{m.message}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* REPORTS TAB */}
      {activeTab === 'reports' && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border-t-4 border-red-500">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-red-600 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><AlertTriangle size={20}/> Content Issues</h3>
          {reportsList.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400">No active reports. Good job!</p>}
          {reportsList.map(m => (
            <div key={m.id} className="border-b border-red-50 py-4 last:border-0">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <span className="bg-red-100 text-red-600 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded mb-2 inline-block">BUG REPORT</span>
                  <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm mb-1">{new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString()}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 font-mono bg-red-50/50 p-3 rounded border border-red-100">{m.message}</p>
                </div>
                <button onClick={() => handleDelete('contact_messages', m.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 ml-4"><Trash2 size={18}/></button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <style>{`
        .label { display: block; font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: bold; color: #4b5563; margin-bottom: 0.3rem; }
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
        .btn-primary { background: #192335; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 0.8rem; border-radius: 0.5rem; transition: opacity 0.2s; }
        .btn-primary:hover { opacity: 0.9; }
        .file-input-wrapper { border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; padding: 0.75rem; border-radius: 0.5rem; background: #f9fafb; transition: background 0.2s; }
        .file-input-wrapper:hover { background: #f3f4f6; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/corsi/[id]/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
// PATH: src/pages/app/CoursePlayer.jsx

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useGamification } from '@/hooks/useGamification'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { 
  PlayCircle, FileDown, Lock, ExternalLink, Loader2, 
  Music, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft, CheckCircle, Circle, 
  Download, HelpCircle, AlertTriangle 
} from 'lucide-react'
import { getEmbedUrl } from '@/lib/video'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'
import html2canvas from 'html2canvas'
import jsPDF from 'jspdf'
import Certificate from '@/components/course/Certificate'
import CustomAudioPlayer from '@/components/course/CustomAudioPlayer'
import LessonNotes from '@/components/course/LessonNotes'
import VocabularyDrill from '@/components/course/VocabularyDrill'
import ReviewSection from '@/components/course/ReviewSection'
import QuizTaker from '@/components/course/QuizTaker'
import ReportModal from '@/components/ui/ReportModal'

export default function CoursePlayer() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addXP } = useGamification()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [hasAccess, setHasAccess] = useState(false)
  const [courseInfo, setCourseInfo] = useState(null)
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState([])
  const [activeLessonIndex, setActiveLessonIndex] = useState(0)
  
  const [completedLessonIds, setCompletedLessonIds] = useState([])
  const [marking, setMarking] = useState(false)
  const [generatingPdf, setGeneratingPdf] = useState(false)
  const [xpAnimation, setXpAnimation] = useState(null)
  const [showQuiz, setShowQuiz] = useState(false)
  
  const [showReport, setShowReport] = useState(false)
  const certificateRef = useRef()

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && id) checkAccessAndFetchContent()
  }, [user, id])

  useEffect(() => {
    setShowQuiz(false)
  }, [activeLessonIndex])

  const checkAccessAndFetchContent = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const { data: product } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('id', id).single()
      setCourseInfo(product)

      const { data: purchase } = await supabase.from('purchases')
        .select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('product_id', id).eq('status', 'completed').single()

      if (purchase) {
        setHasAccess(true)
        const { data: lessonsData } = await supabase.from('lessons')
          .select('*').eq('course_id', id).order('created_at', { ascending: true })
        setLessons(lessonsData || [])

        const { data: progressData } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress')
          .select('lesson_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('course_id', id)
        if (progressData) setCompletedLessonIds(progressData.map(p => p.lesson_id))
      } else {
        setHasAccess(false)
      }
    } catch (err) { console.error(err) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const toggleCompletion = async () => {
    if (!lessons[activeLessonIndex]) return
    const lessonId = lessons[activeLessonIndex].id
    const isComplete = completedLessonIds.includes(lessonId)
    
    setMarking(true)
    try {
      let newCompletedIds;
      if (isComplete) {
        // CASE: UNMARKING (Incomplete)
        await supabase.from('lesson_progress').delete().eq('user_id', user.id).eq('lesson_id', lessonId)
        newCompletedIds = completedLessonIds.filter(id => id !== lessonId)
        
        // 🐛 BUG FIX: Remove 50 XP so users can't farm XP by toggling
        addXP(-50) 

      } else {
        // CASE: MARKING (Complete)
        await supabase.from('lesson_progress').insert([{ user_id: user.id, lesson_id: lessonId, course_id: id }])
        newCompletedIds = [...completedLessonIds, lessonId]
        
        // Add 50 XP
        addXP(50)
        setXpAnimation(50)
        setTimeout(() => setXpAnimation(null), 2000)
        if (newCompletedIds.length === lessons.length) fireConfetti()
      }
      setCompletedLessonIds(newCompletedIds)
    } catch (error) { console.error("Progress Error", error) } 
    finally { setMarking(false) }
  }

  const fireConfetti = () => {
    confetti({ particleCount: 100, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 } })
  }

  const downloadCertificate = async () => {
    if (!certificateRef.current) return
    setGeneratingPdf(true)
    try {
      const canvas = await html2canvas(certificateRef.current, { scale: 2 })
      const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png')
      const pdf = new jsPDF('landscape', 'mm', 'a4')
      pdf.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', 0, 0, 297, 210)
      pdf.save(`Certificate_${courseInfo.title.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.pdf`)
    } catch (err) { alert("Error generating certificate") } 
    finally { setGeneratingPdf(false) }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="h-64 flex items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} /></div>

  if (!hasAccess) return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-[60vh] text-center px-4">
      <Lock className="w-16 h-16 text-gray-300 mb-4" />
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('player_locked')}</h1>
      <button onClick={() => initiateCheckout(courseInfo)} className="bg-brand-purple text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-full font-bold mt-4 hover:bg-brand-dark transition shadow-lg">{t('player_unlock')} {courseInfo?.price}</button>
    </div>
  )

  if (lessons.length === 0) return <div className="text-center py-20">{t('player_no_lessons')}</div>

  const activeLesson = lessons[activeLessonIndex]
  const isLessonCompleted = completedLessonIds.includes(activeLesson.id)
  const progressPercent = Math.round((completedLessonIds.length / lessons.length) * 100)
  const hasQuiz = activeLesson.quiz_data && activeLesson.quiz_data.length > 0

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6 min-h-screen relative">
      <Certificate ref={certificateRef} studentName={profile?.full_name} courseTitle={courseInfo?.title} date={new Date().toLocaleDateString()} />
      {xpAnimation && <div className="fixed top-1/2 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 bg-yellow-400 text-white text-4xl font-black px-8 py-4 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl z-50 animate-bounce border-4 border-white">+{xpAnimation} XP! ⚡</div>}

      <ReportModal 
        isOpen={showReport} 
        onClose={() => setShowReport(false)} 
        contextData={`Course: ${courseInfo.title}, Lesson: ${activeLesson.title}`} 
      />

      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
        
        {hasQuiz && (
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            <button onClick={() => setShowQuiz(false)} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition ${!showQuiz ? 'bg-brand-dark text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'}`}>{t('player_content')}</button>
            <button onClick={() => setShowQuiz(true)} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 ${showQuiz ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'}`}><HelpCircle size={16}/> {t('player_quiz')}</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {showQuiz ? (
          <QuizTaker quizData={activeLesson.quiz_data} onComplete={() => { if(!isLessonCompleted) toggleCompletion() }} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg flex-shrink-0 mb-6">
              {activeLesson.type === 'audio' ? <CustomAudioPlayer src={activeLesson.video_url} title={activeLesson.title} /> : <div className="aspect-video bg-black"><iframe src={getEmbedUrl(activeLesson.video_url)} className="w-full h-full" allowFullScreen title={activeLesson.title} /></div>}
            </div>
            
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mb-8">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{activeLesson.title}</h1>
                <button onClick={toggleCompletion} disabled={marking} className={`flex-shrink-0 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${isLessonCompleted ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200'}`}>
                   {marking ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18}/> : (isLessonCompleted ? <CheckCircle size={18}/> : <Circle size={18}/>)} {isLessonCompleted ? t('player_completed') : t('player_mark_complete')}
                </button>
              </div>
              <VocabularyDrill description={activeLesson.description} />
              <div className="flex gap-3 mb-6">
                 {activeLesson.quizlet_url && <a href={activeLesson.quizlet_url} target="_blank" className="btn-secondary flex gap-2 items-center text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-blue-100"><ExternalLink size={14}/> {t('player_flashcards')}</a>}
                 {activeLesson.pdf_url && <a href={activeLesson.pdf_url} target="_blank" className="btn-secondary flex gap-2 items-center text-sm font-bold text-purple-600 bg-purple-50 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-purple-100"><FileDown size={14}/> {t('player_download_pdf')}</a>}
              </div>
              <div className="prose prose-sm text-gray-600 whitespace-pre-wrap">{cleanDescription(activeLesson.description)}</div>
              
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-6 mt-6 border-t border-gray-100">
                 <button disabled={activeLessonIndex === 0} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(i => i - 1)} className="text-gray-500 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-30 hover:text-brand-dark transition"><ChevronLeft/> {t('player_prev')}</button>
                 
                 <button onClick={() => setShowReport(true)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 text-xs flex items-center gap-1 transition"><AlertTriangle size={12}/> {t('player_report')}</button>
                 
                 <button disabled={activeLessonIndex === lessons.length - 1} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(i => i + 1)} className="text-brand-purple font-bold flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-30 hover:underline transition">{t('player_next')} <ChevronRight/></button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <ReviewSection productId={id} />
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="w-full md:w-80 flex flex-col gap-6 h-fit sticky top-24">
         <div className="h-64 flex-shrink-0"><LessonNotes lessonId={activeLesson.id} userId={user.id} /></div>
         <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col shadow-sm max-h-[500px]">
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg mb-2">{t('player_curriculum')}</h3>
              <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 mb-2"><div className="bg-green-500 h-2 rounded-full transition-all" style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}></div></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-500"><span>{progressPercent}% {t('player_completed')}</span>{progressPercent === 100 && <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={generatingPdf} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">{generatingPdf ? '...' : <><Download size={10}/> {t('player_cert')}</>}</button>}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-y-auto flex-1">
              {lessons.map((l, i) => (
                <button key={l.id} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(i)} className={`w-full text-left p-4 border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50 flex items-center gap-3 ${activeLessonIndex === i ? 'bg-purple-50 border-l-4 border-l-brand-purple' : ''}`}>
                  <div className="text-gray-400">{completedLessonIds.includes(l.id) ? <CheckCircle size={16} className="text-green-500"/> : (l.type === 'audio' ? <Music size={16}/> : <PlayCircle size={16}/>)}</div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 truncate flex-1">{l.title}</div>
                  {l.quiz_data && <div className="text-xs bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 px-1.5 rounded font-bold">Quiz</div>}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
         </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/corsi/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { Video, Loader2, PlayCircle, ShoppingCart, Search } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function Courses() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('')
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData()
  }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'course')
      if (error) throw error
      setCourses(data || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase
          .from('purchases')
          .select('product_id')
          .eq('user_id', user.id)
          .eq('status', 'completed')
        
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const filteredCourses = courses.filter(course => {
    const matchesSearch = course.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
    const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)
    
    if (filter === 'owned' && !isOwned) return false
    if (filter === 'not_owned' && isOwned) return false
    
    return matchesSearch
  })

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('course_catalog')}</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('course_desc')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
          <div className="relative flex-1 md:w-64">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" size={18} />
            <input 
              type="text" 
              placeholder={t('course_search')}
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-brand-purple"
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <select 
            className="px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none bg-white text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold"
            value={filter}
            onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="all">{t('course_filter_all')}</option>
            <option value="owned">{t('course_filter_owned')}</option>
            <option value="not_owned">{t('course_filter_not')}</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {filteredCourses.length === 0 && (
        <div className="text-center py-12 bg-white rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
          <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium">{t('course_no_results')}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {filteredCourses.map(course => {
          const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)

          return (
            <div key={course.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border transition group flex flex-col ${isOwned ? 'border-green-200 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md'}`}>
              <div className="h-48 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-100">
                <img 
                  src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/600x400/6a11cb/ffffff?text=Course'} 
                  alt={course.title} 
                  className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" 
                />
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Video size={12} className="text-blue-500" /> VIDEO
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{course.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4 line-clamp-2 flex-1">{cleanDescription(course.description)}</p>
                
                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                  {isOwned ? (
                     <Link 
                       href={`/corsi/${course.id}`}
                       className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                     >
                       <PlayCircle size={16} /> {t('course_access')}
                     </Link>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple">{course.price}</span>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => initiateCheckout(course)}
                        className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center gap-2"
                      >
                        <ShoppingCart size={16}/> {t('course_enroll')}
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/dashboard/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useGamification } from '@/hooks/useGamification'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { BookOpen, Loader2, PlayCircle, Calendar, Download, ArrowRight, Award, Zap, Flame } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  
  // Destructure safely with default fallback
  const { stats = { xp: 0, streak: 0 }, loading: gameLoading } = useGamification()
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [liveLessons, setLiveLessons] = useState([])
  const [ebooks, setEbooks] = useState([])
  const [progressMap, setProgressMap] = useState({})

  useEffect(() => { if (user) fetchData() }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
      if (purchases && purchases.length > 0) {
        const ids = purchases.map(p => p.product_id)
        const { data: products } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').in('id', ids)
        if (products) {
          const myCourses = products.filter(p => p.type === 'course')
          setCourses(myCourses)
          setLiveLessons(products.filter(p => p.type === 'standalone'))
          setEbooks(products.filter(p => p.type === 'ebook'))

          // Calculate Progress
          const progressData = {}
          const { data: userProgress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('course_id').eq('user_id', user.id)
          const { data: allLessons } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('course_id').in('course_id', myCourses.map(c => c.id))

          myCourses.forEach(course => {
             const total = allLessons.filter(l => l.course_id === course.id).length
             const completed = userProgress.filter(p => p.course_id === course.id).length
             progressData[course.id] = total > 0 ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0
          })
          setProgressMap(progressData)
        }
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  // Combine loading states
  if (loading || gameLoading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  const displayName = profile?.full_name ? profile.full_name.split(' ')[0] : user?.email?.split('@')[0]

  return (
    <div className="pb-10">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">{t('dash_hello')}, {displayName}! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('dash_ready')}</p>
        </div>
        
        {/* Gamification Stats Header */}
        <div className="flex gap-4">
           <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="bg-orange-100 p-1.5 rounded-lg"><Flame size={20} className="text-orange-500 fill-orange-500"/></div>
              <div>
                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase">{t('dash_streak')}</p>
                 <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{stats?.streak || 0} {t('dash_days')}</p>
              </div>
           </div>
           <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="bg-yellow-100 p-1.5 rounded-lg"><Zap size={20} className="text-yellow-500 fill-yellow-500"/></div>
              <div>
                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase">{t('dash_xp')}</p>
                 <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{stats?.xp || 0} XP</p>
              </div>
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-12">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between h-32 hover:shadow-md transition">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
            <div><p className="text-gray-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('dash_my_courses')}</p><p className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-purple mt-1">{courses.length}</p></div>
            <div className="bg-purple-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-purple"><BookOpen size={20}/></div>
          </div>
          <Link href="/corsi" className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-1 transition">{t('dash_view_catalog')} <ArrowRight size={12}/></Link>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between h-32 hover:shadow-md transition">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
            <div><p className="text-gray-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('dash_live_credits')}</p><p className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-blue mt-1">{liveLessons.length}</p></div>
            <div className="bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-blue"><Calendar size={20}/></div>
          </div>
          <Link href="/lezioni-live" className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-blue flex items-center gap-1 transition">{t('dash_book_new')} <ArrowRight size={12}/></Link>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between h-32 hover:shadow-md transition">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
            <div><p className="text-gray-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('dash_files')}</p><p className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-700 mt-1">{ebooks.length}</p></div>
            <div className="bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-600"><Download size={20}/></div>
          </div>
          <Link href="/downloads" className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center gap-1 transition">{t('dash_go_files')} <ArrowRight size={12}/></Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('dash_my_courses')}</h2>
          {courses.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {courses.map(course => {
                const progress = progressMap[course.id] || 0
                return (
                  <div key={course.id} className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md transition group">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                      <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0 relative">
                         <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/150'} alt={course.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 py-1">
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                           <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg truncate pr-2">{course.title}</h3>
                           <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-2 rounded-full shadow-md hover:scale-110 transition transform"><PlayCircle size={20} fill="white" className="text-brand-purple" /></Link>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 line-clamp-1">{course.description.replace(/\[\[.*?\]\]/g, '')}</p>
                        <div className="mt-4">
                           <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold mb-1"><span className={progress === 100 ? "text-green-600" : "text-gray-400"}>{progress === 100 ? "Completed! 🏆" : `${progress}% Complete`}</span></div>
                           <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-2"><div className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-1000 ${progress === 100 ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-brand-purple'}`} style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}></div></div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-10 text-center border border-dashed border-gray-300">
              <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">{t('dash_no_courses')}</p>
              <Link href="/corsi" className="inline-block bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm hover:shadow-lg transition">{t('dash_start_now')}</Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-8">
           <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-dark to-gray-900 rounded-2xl p-6 text-white relative overflow-hidden">
              <div className="relative z-10">
                 <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                    <div className="bg-white/20 p-2 rounded-lg"><Award size={20}/></div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold">{t('dash_league')}</h3>
                 </div>
                 <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm mb-4">Silver League. Keep pushing!</p>
                 <div className="w-full bg-white/10 rounded-full h-2"><div className="bg-yellow-400 h-2 rounded-full" style={{width: '60%'}}></div></div>
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -bottom-4 -right-4 text-white/5"><Award size={120}/></div>
           </div>

           <div>
             <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">{t('dash_upcoming')}</h2>
             {liveLessons.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {liveLessons.slice(0, 2).map(lesson => (
                    <div key={lesson.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-3 items-center">
                      <div className="bg-orange-50 text-orange-500 p-3 rounded-lg h-fit"><Calendar size={18}/></div>
                      <div>
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800 line-clamp-1">{lesson.title}</h4>
                        <a href="https://zoom.us/join" target="_blank" className="text-xs text-brand-blue font-bold hover:underline mt-1 block">{t('dash_join')}</a>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
             ) : (
                <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-6 text-center">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 italic mb-2">{t('dash_no_live')}</p>
                    <Link href="/lezioni-live" className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-blue underline">{t('dash_book_new')}</Link>
                </div>
             )}
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/downloads/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Download, FileText, Loader2, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react'
import { extractTag, cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function MyDownloads() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [downloads, setDownloads] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) fetchDownloads()
  }, [user])

  const fetchDownloads = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
      if (purchases && purchases.length > 0) {
        const ids = purchases.map(p => p.product_id)
        const { data: products } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').in('id', ids).eq('type', 'ebook')
        setDownloads(products || [])
      }
    } catch (e) { console.error(e) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('dl_title')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('dl_desc')}</p>

      {downloads.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white p-12 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-gray-300 text-center">
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">{t('dl_empty')}</p>
          <a href="/store" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">{t('nav_store')}</a>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {downloads.map(item => {
            const downloadUrl = extractTag(item.description, 'DOWNLOAD_URL');
            
            return (
              <div key={item.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-brand-blue flex-shrink-0">
                    <FileText size={28} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg">{item.title}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 line-clamp-1">{cleanDescription(item.description)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                {downloadUrl ? (
                  <a href={downloadUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg">
                    <Download size={18} /> {t('dl_btn')}
                  </a>
                ) : (
                  <button disabled className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold cursor-not-allowed">
                    <ExternalLink size={18} /> Link unavailable
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/layout.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { LayoutDashboard, BookOpen, Calendar, LogOut, User, ShieldCheck, ShoppingBag, Settings, Download, Menu, X } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AppLayout({ children }) {
  const { signOut, user, profile, isAdmin } = useAuth()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false)

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await signOut()
    router.push('/login')
  }

  const NavItem = ({ href, icon: Icon, label }) => {
    const isActive = pathname === href
    return (
      <Link 
        href={href} 
        onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
          isActive ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-md' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-purple-50 hover:text-brand-purple'
        }`}
      >
        <Icon size={20} />
        <span className="font-medium">{label}</span>
      </Link>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      
      <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 left-0 w-full bg-white z-30 px-4 py-3 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple text-lg">Annyeong LMS</span>
        <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} className="text-gray-700">
           {mobileOpen ? <X size={24}/> : <Menu size={24}/>}
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-20 md:hidden" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}></div>
      )}

      <aside className={`fixed top-0 left-0 h-full w-64 bg-white border-r border-gray-200 z-30 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:translate-x-0 ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
        <div className="p-6 hidden md:block">
          <span className="text-xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple">
            Annyeong LMS
          </span>
        </div>
        
        <div className="md:hidden p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
           <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Menu</span>
           <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 px-4 space-y-2 mt-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <NavItem href="/dashboard" icon={LayoutDashboard} label="Dashboard" />
          <NavItem href="/corsi" icon={BookOpen} label="All Courses" />
          <NavItem href="/lezioni-live" icon={Calendar} label="Live Lessons" />
          <NavItem href="/store" icon={ShoppingBag} label="Store & Ebooks" />
          <NavItem href="/downloads" icon={Download} label="My Downloads" />
          <NavItem href="/profile" icon={Settings} label="Profile" />
          
          {isAdmin && (
            <div className="pt-4 mt-4 border-t border-gray-100">
               <p className="px-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">Admin Area</p>
               <NavItem href="/admin" icon={ShieldCheck} label="Management" />
            </div>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 mb-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 overflow-hidden">
              {profile?.avatar_url ? (
                <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Profile" className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
              ) : (
                <User size={16} />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm overflow-hidden">
              <p className="font-bold text-gray-700 truncate">{profile?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleLogout} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition text-sm font-medium">
            <LogOut size={18} /> Logout
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="flex-1 md:ml-64 pt-16 md:pt-0">
        <main className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
