PATH: ./src/app/(app)/lezioni-live/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Calendar, Loader2, Users, Video, CheckCircle, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { extractTag, cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function LiveLessons() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData()
  }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: products, error } = await supabase
        .from('products')
        .select('*')
        .eq('type', 'standalone')
        .order('event_date', { ascending: true, nullsLast: true })
      
      if (error) throw error
      setLessons(products || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('live_schedule')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('live_desc')}</p>
      
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {lessons.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 italic">{t('dash_no_live')}</p>}

        {lessons.map(lesson => {
          const isPurchased = purchasedIds.includes(lesson.id)
          const zoomLink = extractTag(lesson.description, 'ZOOM') || "https://zoom.us/join"
          const eventDate = lesson.event_date ? new Date(lesson.event_date) : null
          const isExpired = eventDate && eventDate < new Date()
          
          return (
            <div key={lesson.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 border transition relative ${isPurchased ? 'border-green-200 bg-green-50/30' : 'border-gray-100 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'} ${isExpired ? 'opacity-60 grayscale' : ''}`}>
              
              {isExpired && (
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <AlertTriangle size={12} /> {t('live_expired')}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6 items-center">
                <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl flex-shrink-0 w-full md:w-24 text-center ${isPurchased ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-600' : 'bg-purple-50 text-brand-purple'}`}>
                  {eventDate ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">{eventDate.toLocaleString('default', { month: 'short' })}</span>
                      <span className="block text-3xl font-black leading-none my-1">{eventDate.getDate()}</span>
                      <span className="block text-xs font-bold">{eventDate.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-2">
                      <Calendar size={32} />
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold mt-2">{t('live_tba')}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-left">
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{lesson.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-600 mb-3">{cleanDescription(lesson.description)}</p>
                  
                  <div className="flex justify-center md:justify-start gap-4 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Users size={16}/> Small Group</span>
                    {isPurchased && <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-green-600 font-bold bg-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shadow-sm border border-green-100"><CheckCircle size={14}/> {t('live_confirmed')}</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="text-center min-w-[180px]">
                  {isPurchased ? (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <a 
                        href={isExpired ? '#' : zoomLink} 
                        target={isExpired ? '' : "_blank"} 
                        className={`block w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg ${isExpired ? 'bg-gray-300 cursor-not-allowed text-gray-500' : 'bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700 shadow-green-200'}`}
                      >
                        <Video size={18} /> {isExpired ? t('live_expired') : t('live_join')}
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple mb-3">{lesson.price}</div>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => initiateCheckout(lesson)} 
                        disabled={isExpired}
                        className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                      >
                        {isExpired ? t('live_closed') : t('live_book')}
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/profile/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { User, Save, Loader2, Camera, Lock, FileText, Download } from 'lucide-react'
import { generateFilePath } from '@/lib/helpers'
import { generateInvoice } from '@/utils/invoiceGenerator'

export default function Profile() {
  const { user, refreshProfile } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [passwordLoading, setPasswordLoading] = useState(false)
  
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('')
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState('')
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)

  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('')
  const [passwordMsg, setPasswordMsg] = useState('')

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      getProfile()
      getOrders()
    }
  }, [user])

  const getProfile = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('full_name, avatar_url').eq('id', user.id).single()
      if (data) {
        setFullName(data.full_name || '')
        setAvatarUrl(data.avatar_url || '')
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) }
  }

  const getOrders = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('purchases')
        .select('id, created_at, products(title, price)')
        .eq('user_id', user.id)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      setOrders(data || [])
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) }
  }

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (event) => {
    try {
      setUploading(true)
      const file = event.target.files[0]
      if (!file) return
      const path = generateFilePath('avatars', file)
      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from('avatars').upload(path, file)
      if (uploadError) throw uploadError
      const { data } = supabase.storage.from('avatars').getPublicUrl(path)
      setAvatarUrl(data.publicUrl)
    } catch (error) { alert('Upload error: ' + error.message) } 
    finally { setUploading(false) }
  }

  const updateProfile = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    try {
      setLoading(true)
      const updates = { id: user.id, full_name: fullName, avatar_url: avatarUrl, updated_at: new Date() }
      const { error } = await supabase.from('profiles').upsert(updates)
      if (error) throw error
      await refreshProfile() 
      alert('Profile updated successfully!')
    } catch (error) { alert(error.message) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handlePasswordUpdate = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setPasswordMsg('')
    if (newPassword.length < 6) return setPasswordMsg('Password must be at least 6 characters.')
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) return setPasswordMsg('Passwords do not match.')
    setPasswordLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: newPassword })
    if (error) setPasswordMsg('Error: ' + error.message)
    else {
      setPasswordMsg('Success: Password updated successfully!')
      setNewPassword('')
      setConfirmPassword('')
    }
    setPasswordLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('prof_title')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('prof_desc')}</p>

      <div className="grid gap-8">
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple"></div>
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-8 mb-8">
            <div className="relative group">
              <div className="w-32 h-32 bg-purple-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden border-4 border-white shadow-lg">
                 {avatarUrl ? <img src={avatarUrl} alt="Avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <User size={48} className="text-brand-purple opacity-50" />}
                 {uploading && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-white"/></div>}
              </div>
              <label className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-brand-dark text-white p-2 rounded-full cursor-pointer hover:bg-brand-purple transition shadow-md">
                 <Camera size={18} />
                 <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={handleAvatarUpload} disabled={uploading} />
              </label>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center md:text-left">
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">{t('prof_student')}</p>
              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{fullName || "Unnamed User"}</p>
              <p className="text-gray-400">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <form onSubmit={updateProfile} className="space-y-6 border-t border-gray-100 pt-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">{t('auth_fullname')}</label>
              <input type="text" value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none bg-gray-50 focus:bg-white transition" />
            </div>
            <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="w-full md:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-lg">
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <><Save size={20} /> {t('prof_save')}</>}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-600"><Lock size={20} /></div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('prof_security')}</h3>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handlePasswordUpdate} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">{t('prof_new_pass')}</label>
                <input type="password" value={newPassword} onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none" placeholder="••••••••" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">{t('prof_confirm_pass')}</label>
                <input type="password" value={confirmPassword} onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none" placeholder="••••••••" />
              </div>
              {passwordMsg && <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold ${passwordMsg.startsWith('Success') ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-600' : 'bg-red-50 text-red-600'}`}>{passwordMsg}</div>}
              <button disabled={passwordLoading || !newPassword} type="submit" className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-lg disabled:opacity-50">
                {passwordLoading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto" size={20} /> : t('prof_update')}
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-blue"><FileText size={20} /></div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('prof_orders')}</h3>
            </div>
            {orders.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
                {orders.map(order => (
                  <div key={order.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{order.products?.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString()} • {order.products?.price}</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => generateInvoice(order, {full_name: fullName})} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple transition" title="Download Invoice">
                      <Download size={18} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-gray-400 italic text-sm">{t('prof_no_orders')}</p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/app/(app)/store/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { ShoppingBag, Loader2, Download, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function Store() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData()
  }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'ebook')
      if (error) throw error
      setProducts(data || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase
          .from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('store_title')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('store_desc')}</p>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {products.map(product => {
          const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(product.id)
          return (
            <div key={product.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border transition group flex flex-col ${isOwned ? 'border-green-200 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'}`}>
              <div className="h-56 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                <img src={product.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400x500/2575fc/ffffff?text=Ebook'} alt={product.title} className={`h-full object-cover transition duration-500 ${!isOwned && 'group-hover:scale-105'}`} />
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple shadow-sm">EBOOK</div>
                {isOwned && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-[2px]"><div className="bg-green-500 text-white w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg"><Check size={24} strokeWidth={3} /></div></div>}
              </div>
              <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{product.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4 line-clamp-2 flex-1">{cleanDescription(product.description)}</p>
                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                  {isOwned ? (
                    <Link href="/downloads" className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-green-100 transition"><Download size={16} /> {t('store_download')}</Link>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <span className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-purple">{product.price}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => initiateCheckout(product)} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-brand-dark text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-md"><ShoppingBag size={16} /> {t('store_buy')}</button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/contact/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Mail, Phone, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function Contact() {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: '', email: '', message: '' })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([formData])
      setSent(true)
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Error sending message")
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-12">
          
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6">{t('contact_title')}</h1>
            <p className="text-gray-600 mb-8">{t('contact_desc')}</p>
            
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-brand-purple"><Mail /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Email</p>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">info@annyeong-academy.com</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-brand-blue"><Phone /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Phone</p>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">+36 30 123 4567</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-lg">
            {sent ? (
              <div className="text-center py-12">
                <div className="text-5xl mb-4">✅</div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('contact_sent')}</h3>
                <p className="text-gray-500">{t('contact_reply')}</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('contact_name')}</label>
                  <input 
                    required 
                    type="text" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.name}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, name: e.target.value})}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('auth_email')}</label>
                  <input 
                    required 
                    type="email" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.email}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('contact_msg')}</label>
                  <textarea 
                    required 
                    rows="4" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.message}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, message: e.target.value})}
                  ></textarea>
                </div>
                <button 
                  disabled={loading}
                  type="submit" 
                  className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:opacity-90 transition flex justify-center"
                >
                  {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : t('btn_send')}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>

        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/forgot-password/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)

  const handleReset = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
      redirectTo: window.location.origin + '/profile',
    })
    setLoading(false)
    if (error) {
      alert(error.message)
    } else {
      setSent(true)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-gray-800">
        <Link href="/login" className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 text-sm">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to Login
        </Link>

        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Reset Password 🔒</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Enter your email to recover access.</p>
        </div>
        
        {sent ? (
          <div className="text-center bg-green-50 p-6 rounded-xl">
            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold mb-2">Check your email!</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-600">We sent you a link to reset your password.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleReset} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
              <input 
                type="email" 
                required
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            
            <button 
              disabled={loading}
              type="submit" 
              className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Send Reset Link"}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

}

  list-style: none;
}

  display: none;
}

  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}

  animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease-out forwards;
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/login/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const { signIn } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()

  const handleLogin = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const { data, error } = await signIn({ email, password })
    
    if (error) {
      alert("Login error: " + error.message)
    } else {
      const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('role').eq('id', data.user.id).single()
      if (profile?.role === 'admin') {
        router.push('/admin')
      } else {
        router.push('/dashboard')
      }
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-brand-purple">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Welcome Back! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Access your student area</p>
        </div>
        
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
            <input 
              type="email" 
              required
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Password</label>
              <Link href="/forgot-password" class="text-xs text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Forgot?</Link>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="password" 
              required
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          
          <button 
            disabled={loading}
            type="submit" 
            className="w-full bg-brand-purple text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Login"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="text-center mt-6 text-sm text-gray-600">
          Don't have an account? <Link href="/register" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Register</Link>
        </p>
        <p className="text-center mt-2 text-sm">
          <Link href="/" className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">← Back to Home</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/register/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, User, Mail, Lock } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Register() {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    fullName: '',
    email: '',
    password: ''
  })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const { signUp } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()

  const handleRegister = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    const { error } = await signUp({ 
      email: formData.email, 
      password: formData.password,
      options: {
        data: {
          full_name: formData.fullName,
          avatar_url: ""
        }
      }
    })

    if (error) {
      alert("Registration error: " + error.message)
    } else {
      alert("Registration successful! Please check your email to confirm your account.")
      router.push('/login')
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-brand-blue">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Create Account 🚀</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Start learning Korean today</p>
        </div>
        
        <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Full Name</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><User size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="text" 
                required
                placeholder="e.g. John Doe"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.fullName}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, fullName: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><Mail size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="email" 
                required
                placeholder="john@example.com"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.email}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Password</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><Lock size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="password" 
                required
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.password}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, password: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <button 
            disabled={loading}
            type="submit" 
            className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center transition transform active:scale-95"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Register Free"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="text-center mt-6 text-sm text-gray-600">
          Already have an account? <Link href="/login" className="text-brand-blue font-bold hover:underline">Login</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

          <p className="text-xl text-gray-600">
            {t('tutors_desc')}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
          {tutors.map(tutor => (
            <div key={tutor.id} className="bg-white rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg hover:-translate-y-2 transition duration-300">
              <div className="h-64 overflow-hidden bg-gray-100">
                <img src={tutor.image} alt={tutor.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
              </div>
              <div className="p-8">
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">{tutor.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider mb-4">{tutor.role}</p>
                <p className="text-gray-600 mb-6 text-sm leading-relaxed">
                  {tutor.bio}
                </p>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {tutor.specialties.map(tag => (
                    <span key={tag} className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">
                      #{tag}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AdminRoute() {
  const { user, isAdmin, loading } = useAuth()

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!user || !isAdmin) {
    return <Navigate href="/dashboard" replace />
  }

  return <Outlet />
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/CustomAudioPlayer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Play, Pause, RotateCcw, MoreHorizontal } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CustomAudioPlayer({ src, title }) {
  const audioRef = useRef(null)
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false)
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0)
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState(1)

  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = audioRef.current
    if(!audio) return

    const updateProgress = () => setProgress((audio.currentTime / audio.duration) * 100)
    const setAudioDuration = () => setDuration(audio.duration)
    const onEnd = () => setIsPlaying(false)

    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', updateProgress)
    audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', setAudioDuration)
    audio.addEventListener('ended', onEnd)

    return () => {
      audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', updateProgress)
      audio.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', setAudioDuration)
      audio.removeEventListener('ended', onEnd)
    }
  }, [src])

  const togglePlay = () => {
    if (isPlaying) audioRef.current.pause()
    else audioRef.current.play()
    setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)
  }

  const changeSpeed = () => {
    const speeds = [0.8, 1, 1.25, 1.5]
    const nextIdx = (speeds.indexOf(speed) + 1) % speeds.length
    const nextSpeed = speeds[nextIdx]
    audioRef.current.playbackRate = nextSpeed
    setSpeed(nextSpeed)
  }

  const handleSeek = (e) => {
    const width = e.target.clientWidth
    const clickX = e.nativeEvent.offsetX
    const duration = audioRef.current.duration
    audioRef.current.currentTime = (clickX / width) * duration
  }

  const formatTime = (time) => {
    if(isNaN(time)) return "0:00"
    const min = Math.floor(time / 60)
    const sec = Math.floor(time % 60)
    return `${min}:${sec < 10 ? '0'+sec : sec}`
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-lg">
      <audio ref={audioRef} src={src} />
      
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-brand-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 animate-pulse-slow">
          <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full"></div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg leading-tight">{title}</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Audio Lesson</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 cursor-pointer group py-2" onClick={handleSeek}>
        <div className="w-full bg-gray-700 rounded-full h-2 relative">
          <div 
            className="bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple h-2 rounded-full relative" 
            style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
          >
            <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full absolute right-0 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition shadow-md"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1 font-mono">
          <span>{formatTime(audioRef.current?.currentTime)}</span>
          <span>{formatTime(duration)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <button 
          onClick={changeSpeed}
          className="text-xs font-bold bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg transition w-16"
        >
          {speed}x
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <button onClick={() => { audioRef.current.currentTime -= 10 }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition">
            <RotateCcw size={20} />
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={togglePlay}
            className="w-14 h-14 bg-white text-brand-dark rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:scale-105 transition shadow-lg shadow-white/10"
          >
            {isPlaying ? <Pause size={24} fill="currentColor" /> : <Play size={24} fill="currentColor" className="ml-1" />}
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => { audioRef.current.currentTime += 10 }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition transform scale-x-[-1]">
            <RotateCcw size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="w-16"></div> {/* Spacer for centering */}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/LessonNotes.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Save, StickyNote, Loader2, Cloud, CloudOff } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function LessonNotes({ lessonId, userId }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle')

  useEffect(() => { fetchNote() }, [lessonId])

  const fetchNote = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('user_notes').select('content').eq('user_id', userId).eq('lesson_id', lessonId).single()
      if (data) setNote(data.content)
      else setNote('')
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  const saveNote = async (content) => {
    setStatus('saving')
    try {
      await supabase.from('user_notes').upsert({ user_id: userId, lesson_id: lessonId, content, updated_at: new Date() }, { onConflict: 'user_id, lesson_id' })
      setStatus('saved')
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('idle'), 2000)
    } catch (error) { setStatus('error') }
  }

  const handleManualSave = () => saveNote(note)

  return (
    <div className="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-100 rounded-2xl p-4 h-full flex flex-col relative">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <h4 className="text-yellow-800 font-bold flex items-center gap-2"><StickyNote size={18} /> {t('notes_title')}</h4>
        <div className="text-xs font-medium">
          {status === 'saving' && <span className="text-yellow-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Loader2 size={12} className="animate-spin"/> {t('notes_saving')}</span>}
          {status === 'saved' && <span className="text-green-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Cloud size={12}/> {t('notes_saved')}</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
      <textarea 
        className="flex-1 w-full bg-transparent border-none outline-none resize-none text-gray-700 text-sm leading-relaxed font-medium placeholder-yellow-800/30 focus:ring-0"
        placeholder={t('notes_placeholder')}
        value={note}
        onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={handleManualSave}
      ></textarea>
      <button onClick={handleManualSave} className="mt-2 w-full bg-yellow-200 text-yellow-900 text-sm font-bold py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-yellow-300 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
        <Save size={16} /> {t('notes_save')}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/QuizTaker.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, RefreshCw, Trophy } from 'lucide-react'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function QuizTaker({ quizData, onComplete }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState(0)
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0)
  const [showScore, setShowScore] = useState(false)
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState(null)
  const [isAnswered, setIsAnswered] = useState(false)

  if (!quizData || quizData.length === 0) return null

  const handleAnswer = (optionIndex) => {
    setSelectedOption(optionIndex)
    setIsAnswered(true)
    if (optionIndex === quizData[currentQuestion].correct) {
      setScore(score + 1)
      confetti({ particleCount: 30, spread: 50, origin: { y: 0.7 } })
    }
  }

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentQuestion < quizData.length - 1) {
      setCurrentQuestion(currentQuestion + 1)
      setSelectedOption(null)
      setIsAnswered(false)
    } else {
      setShowScore(true)
      if (onComplete) onComplete(score)
    }
  }

  const restart = () => {
    setCurrentQuestion(0)
    setScore(0)
    setShowScore(false)
    setSelectedOption(null)
    setIsAnswered(false)
  }

  if (showScore) {
    const passed = score / quizData.length >= 0.7
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 text-center border-2 border-gray-100">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto bg-gray-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          <Trophy size={40} className={passed ? "text-yellow-500" : "text-gray-400"} />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('quiz_completed')}</h3>
        <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">{t('quiz_scored')} {score} / {quizData.length}</p>
        <button onClick={restart} className="bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto">
          <RefreshCw size={18} /> {t('quiz_retry')}
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const question = quizData[currentQuestion]

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple uppercase tracking-wider">
          {t('quiz_question')} {currentQuestion + 1} / {quizData.length}
        </span>
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">{t('quiz_score')}: {score}</span>
      </div>

      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6">{question.question}</h3>

      <div className="space-y-3 mb-8">
        {question.options.map((option, index) => {
          let btnClass = "w-full p-4 text-left rounded-xl border-2 transition font-medium "
          if (isAnswered) {
            if (index === question.correct) btnClass += "border-green-500 bg-green-50 text-green-700"
            else if (index === selectedOption) btnClass += "border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-700"
            else btnClass += "border-gray-100 text-gray-400"
          } else {
            btnClass += "border-gray-100 hover:border-brand-purple hover:bg-purple-50 text-gray-700"
          }

          return (
            <button key={index} disabled={isAnswered} onClick={() => handleAnswer(index)} className={btnClass}>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span>{option}</span>
                {isAnswered && index === question.correct && <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-500"/>}
                {isAnswered && index === selectedOption && index !== question.correct && <XCircle size={20} className="text-red-500"/>}
              </div>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      {isAnswered && (
        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition">
            {currentQuestion === quizData.length - 1 ? t('quiz_finish') : t('quiz_next')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/ReviewSection.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Star, Loader2, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useToast } from '@/context/ToastContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function ReviewSection({ productId }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(5)
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('')
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [hasReviewed, setHasReviewed] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchReviews() }, [productId])

  const fetchReviews = async () => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('reviews').select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)').eq('product_id', productId).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      if (error) throw error
      setReviews(data)
      if (user) setHasReviewed(data.some(r => r.user_id === user.id))
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!comment.trim()) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('reviews').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId, rating, comment }])
      if (error) throw error
      addToast("Review submitted!", "success")
      setComment('')
      setHasReviewed(true)
      fetchReviews()
    } catch (error) { addToast(error.message, "error") } finally { setSubmitting(false) }
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm("Delete your review?")) return
    await supabase.from('reviews').delete().eq('id', id)
    setHasReviewed(false)
    fetchReviews()
  }

  const avgRating = reviews.length ? (reviews.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.rating, 0) / reviews.length).toFixed(1) : 0

  if (loading) return <div className="py-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mt-8">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-4 rounded-2xl text-center min-w-[100px]">
          <span className="block text-3xl font-black text-brand-dark">{avgRating}</span>
          <div className="flex justify-center text-yellow-400 text-sm">{[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={12} fill={i < Math.round(avgRating) ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}</div>
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold mt-1">{reviews.length} Reviews</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('review_title')}</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('review_subtitle')}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!hasReviewed && user && (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mb-8 bg-gray-50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-700 mb-4">{t('review_write')}</h4>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(star => (
              <button type="button" key={star} onClick={() => setRating(star)} className="focus:outline-none transition transform hover:scale-110">
                <Star size={24} className={star <= rating ? "text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" : "text-gray-300"} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <textarea 
            className="w-full p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple text-sm"
            placeholder={t('review_placeholder')} rows="3" value={comment} onChange={e => setComment(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button disabled={submitting} className="mt-3 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm hover:opacity-90 transition">
            {submitting ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : t('review_submit')}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {reviews.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-center italic">{t('review_no_reviews')}</p>}
        {reviews.map(review => (
          <div key={review.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 pb-6 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <img src={review.profiles?.avatar_url || 'https://placehold.co/50'} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover bg-gray-200" />
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{review.profiles?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
                  <div className="flex text-yellow-400 text-xs">{[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={10} fill={i < review.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              {user?.id === review.user_id && <button onClick={() => handleDelete(review.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 transition"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>}
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm leading-relaxed mt-2">{review.comment}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/VocabularyDrill.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { RefreshCw, Check, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function VocabularyDrill({ description }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [vocab, setVocab] = useState([])
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)
  const [showTranslation, setShowTranslation] = useState(false)
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const regex = /\[\[VOCAB::(.*?)\|(.*?)\]\]/g
    const matches = []
    let match
    while ((match = regex.exec(description)) !== null) {
      matches.push({ term: match[1], definition: match[2] })
    }
    setVocab(matches)
    setCurrentIndex(0)
    setFinished(false)
    setShowTranslation(false)
  }, [description])

  const handleNext = () => {
    setShowTranslation(false)
    if (currentIndex < vocab.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex(currentIndex + 1)
    } else {
      setFinished(true)
    }
  }

  const reset = () => {
    setCurrentIndex(0)
    setFinished(false)
    setShowTranslation(false)
  }

  if (vocab.length === 0) return null

  if (finished) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500 to-blue-500 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-white shadow-lg mb-6">
        <div className="text-4xl mb-2">🎉</div>
        <h3 className="font-bold text-xl mb-4">{t('vocab_complete')}</h3>
        <button onClick={reset} className="bg-white text-brand-purple px-6 py-2 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-100 transition flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto">
          <RefreshCw size={16} /> {t('vocab_retry')}
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const currentCard = vocab[currentIndex]

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border-2 border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-6 mb-6 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gray-100">
        <div className="h-1 bg-brand-purple transition-all duration-300" style={{ width: `${((currentIndex + 1) / vocab.length) * 100}%` }}></div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
        <span>{t('vocab_title')}</span>
        <span>{currentIndex + 1} / {vocab.length}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="h-32 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer mb-6 perspective-1000" onClick={() => setShowTranslation(!showTranslation)}>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{currentCard.term}</h2>
        <p className={`text-xl text-brand-blue font-medium transition-all duration-300 ${showTranslation ? 'opacity-100 translate-y-0' : 'opacity-0 translate-y-4'}`}>
          {currentCard.definition}
        </p>
        {!showTranslation && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-4 animate-bounce">{t('vocab_reveal')}</p>}
      </div>

      {showTranslation && (
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="flex-1 bg-red-50 text-red-600 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-red-100 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            <X size={18} /> {t('vocab_hard')}
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="flex-1 bg-green-50 text-green-600 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-green-100 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            <Check size={18} /> {t('vocab_easy')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/layout/AppLayout.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { LayoutDashboard, BookOpen, Calendar, LogOut, User, ShieldCheck, ShoppingBag, Settings, Download, Menu, X, Trophy, StickyNote } from 'lucide-react'
import AnnouncementBanner from '../ui/AnnouncementBanner'

export default function AppLayout() {
  const { signOut, user, profile, isAdmin } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false)

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await signOut()
    router.push('/login')
  }

  const NavItem = ({ to, icon: Icon, label }) => {
    const isActive = pathname === to
    return (
      <Link 
        href={to} 
        onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
          isActive ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-md' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-purple-50 hover:text-brand-purple'
        }`}
      >
        <Icon size={20} />
        <span className="font-medium">{label}</span>
      </Link>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 left-0 w-full bg-white z-30 px-4 py-3 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple text-lg">Annyeong LMS</span>
        <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} className="text-gray-700"><X size={24} className={!mobileOpen && "hidden"}/><Menu size={24} className={mobileOpen && "hidden"}/></button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-20 md:hidden" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}></div>}

      <aside className={`fixed top-0 left-0 h-full w-64 bg-white border-r border-gray-200 z-30 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:translate-x-0 ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
        <div className="p-6 hidden md:block">
          <span className="text-xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple">Annyeong LMS</span>
        </div>
        
        <div className="md:hidden p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
           <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Menu</span>
           <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 px-4 space-y-2 mt-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <NavItem href="/dashboard" icon={LayoutDashboard} label={t('nav_dashboard')} />
          <NavItem href="/corsi" icon={BookOpen} label={t('nav_courses')} />
          <NavItem href="/notebook" icon={StickyNote} label={t('nav_notebook')} />
          <NavItem href="/leaderboard" icon={Trophy} label={t('nav_leaderboard')} />
          <NavItem href="/lezioni-live" icon={Calendar} label={t('nav_live')} />
          <NavItem href="/store" icon={ShoppingBag} label={t('nav_store')} />
          <NavItem href="/downloads" icon={Download} label={t('nav_downloads')} />
          <NavItem href="/profile" icon={Settings} label={t('nav_profile')} />
          
          {isAdmin && (
            <div className="pt-4 mt-4 border-t border-gray-100">
               <p className="px-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">{t('nav_admin')}</p>
               <NavItem href="/admin" icon={ShieldCheck} label={t('nav_management')} />
            </div>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 mb-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 overflow-hidden">
              {profile?.avatar_url ? <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Profile" className="w-full h-full object-cover"/> : <User size={16} />}
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm overflow-hidden">
              <p className="font-bold text-gray-700 truncate">{profile?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleLogout} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition text-sm font-medium">
            <LogOut size={18} /> {t('nav_logout')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="flex-1 md:ml-64 pt-16 md:pt-0 flex flex-col min-h-screen">
        <AnnouncementBanner />
        <main className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto w-full flex-1">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/payment/PaymentModal.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { X, CreditCard, Lock, CheckCircle, Loader2, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useToast } from '@/context/ToastContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function PaymentModal({ isOpen, onClose, item }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [step, setStep] = useState('form') 

  useEffect(() => { if (isOpen) setStep('form') }, [isOpen])

  if (!isOpen || !item) return null

  const handlePayment = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    setTimeout(async () => {
      setStep('processing')
      setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const { error } = await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: item.id, status: 'completed' }])
          if (error) throw error
          setStep('success')
          addToast('Purchase successful!', 'success')
          setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 2000)
        } catch (error) {
          setLoading(false)
          setStep('form')
          addToast(error.message, 'error')
        }
      }, 1500)
    }, 1000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-brand-dark/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full overflow-hidden relative animate-slide-in">
        <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold"><ShieldCheck className="text-green-500" size={20} /> {t('pay_secure')}</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6">
          {step === 'form' && (
            <form onSubmit={handlePayment}>
              <div className="flex gap-4 mb-6">
                <img src={item.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-20 h-20 rounded-lg object-cover bg-gray-100" />
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 leading-tight">{item.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold text-xl">{item.price}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_card')}</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input type="text" placeholder="4242 4242 4242 4242" className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="4242 4242 4242 4242" />
                    <CreditCard className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400" size={18} />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_expiry')}</label>
                    <input type="text" placeholder="MM/YY" className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="12/28" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_cvc')}</label>
                    <input type="text" placeholder="123" className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="123" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg">
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : <><Lock size={16} /> {t('pay_btn')} {item.price}</>}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          {step === 'processing' && (
            <div className="py-10 text-center">
              <Loader2 className="w-16 h-16 text-brand-purple animate-spin mx-auto mb-4" />
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{t('pay_processing')}</h3>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'success' && (
            <div className="py-10 text-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 animate-bounce">
                <CheckCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-green-600" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{t('pay_success')}</h3>
              <p className="text-gray-500">{t('pay_redirect')}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/Countdown.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import Section from '../ui/Section'

export default function Countdown() {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState({ days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    // Target: Dec 14, 11:00 AM (Current Year)
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const targetDate = new Date(`December 14, ${currentYear} 11:00:00`).getTime();

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date().getTime();
      const distance = targetDate - now;

      if (distance < 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
      } else {
        setTimeLeft({
          days: Math.floor(distance / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)),
          hours: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60)),
          minutes: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60)),
          seconds: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000)
        });
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const scrollToForm = () => {
    document.getElementById('registration').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return (
    <section className="bg-brand-dark text-white py-8 px-4 text-center">
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-6">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-brand-blue mb-1">Don't miss out!</p>
          <p className="text-xl font-medium">The trial class starts in:</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-4 text-center">
          {[
            { label: 'Days', value: timeLeft.days },
            { label: 'Hours', value: timeLeft.hours },
            { label: 'Mins', value: timeLeft.minutes },
            { label: 'Secs', value: timeLeft.seconds }
          ].map((item, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="bg-white/10 rounded-lg p-3 min-w-[70px] backdrop-blur-sm">
              <span className="block text-2xl font-bold font-mono">{item.value}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase">{item.label}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <button 
          onClick={scrollToForm}
          className="bg-brand-purple hover:bg-purple-600 text-white font-bold py-3 px-6 rounded-full transition shadow-lg hover:shadow-purple-500/25 whitespace-nowrap"
        >
          Reserve my spot now!
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/Navbar.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Menu, X, LogIn, LayoutDashboard } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function Navbar() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false)
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false)
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 50)
    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
  }, [])

  const scrollToForm = async () => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(false)
    
    // If not on home page, go there first
    if (pathname !== '/') {
      router.push('/')
      // Small timeout to allow page load before scrolling
      // In Next.js, we might need a more robust way, but this works for simple cases
      setTimeout(() => {
        const form = document.getElementById('registration')
        if (form) form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
      }, 500)
    } else {
      // Already on home page
      const form = document.getElementById('registration')
      if (form) form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
    }
  }

  return (
    <nav className={`fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-50 transition-all duration-300 ${scrolled ? 'bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm py-3' : 'bg-transparent py-5'}`}>
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6 flex justify-between items-center">
        
        <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-2xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple">
            Annyeong Academy
          </span>
        </Link>

        <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-8">
          <Link href="/about" className="text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple font-medium transition">About Us</Link>
          <Link href="/tutors" className="text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple font-medium transition">Teachers</Link>
          
          <div className="h-6 w-px bg-gray-300"></div>
          
          {user ? (
            <Link href="/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold hover:text-brand-purple transition">
              <div className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-1 rounded-md"><LayoutDashboard size={16} /></div>
              Dashboard
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <Link href="/login" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold hover:text-brand-purple transition">
              <LogIn size={18} /> Login
            </Link>
          )}
          
          <button 
            onClick={scrollToForm}
            className="px-6 py-2 bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-full font-semibold hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl"
          >
            Book Webinar
          </button>
        </div>

        <button className="md:hidden text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}>
          {mobileMenuOpen ? <X size={28} /> : <Menu size={28} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="md:hidden absolute top-full left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-100 shadow-xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Link href="/about" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>About Us</Link>
          <Link href="/tutors" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Teachers</Link>
          
          {user ? (
             <Link href="/dashboard" className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
                <LayoutDashboard size={20} /> Dashboard
             </Link>
          ) : (
             <Link href="/login" className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
                <LogIn size={20} /> Login
             </Link>
          )}

          <button 
            onClick={scrollToForm}
            className="w-full py-3 bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-xl font-bold mt-2"
          >
            Book Webinar
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </nav>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/StickyBar.jsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'

export default function StickyBar() {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      // Show only after scrolling past header (100px)
      if (window.scrollY > 100) {
        setIsVisible(true);
      } else {
        setIsVisible(false);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  const scrollToForm = () => {
    document.getElementById('registration').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  return (
    <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-200 p-4 shadow-[0_-4px_10px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] z-50 flex items-center justify-between animate-slide-up">
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-dark">
        Free Korean Trial + <br/> AI Translation
      </div>
      <button 
        onClick={scrollToForm}
        className="bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold shadow-md"
      >
        Sign Up
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}
