"use client";
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'

export default function SEO({ title, description }) {
  const pathname = usePathname()

  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = title ? `${title} | Annyeong Academy` : 'Annyeong Academy'
    
    const metaDesc = document.querySelector('meta[name="description"]')
    if (metaDesc) {
      metaDesc.setAttribute('content', description || 'Learn Korean naturally.')
    }
  }, [title, description, location])

  return null
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/AnnouncementBanner.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Megaphone, X } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AnnouncementBanner() {
  const [announcement, setAnnouncement] = useState(null)
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAnnouncement = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('announcements')
        .select('*')
        .eq('active', true)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
      
      if (data && data.length > 0) setAnnouncement(data[0])
    }
    fetchAnnouncement()
  }, [])

  if (!announcement || !visible) return null

  const colors = {
    info: 'bg-blue-600',
    warning: 'bg-orange-500',
    success: 'bg-green-600'
  }

  return (
    <div className={`${colors[announcement.type] || colors.info} text-white px-4 py-3 relative shadow-md`}>
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-start md:items-center gap-3 pr-8">
        <Megaphone size={20} className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 md:mt-0" />
        <div>
          <span className="font-bold mr-2">{announcement.title}:</span>
          <span className="text-sm md:text-base opacity-90">{announcement.message}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button 
        onClick={() => setVisible(false)} 
        className="absolute right-2 top-2 p-1 hover:bg-white/20 rounded-full transition"
      >
        <X size={16} />
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/LegalNotice.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function LegalNotice() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false)
  const { t } = useLanguage()

  useEffect(() => {
    const consent = localStorage.getItem('cookie_consent')
    if (!consent) setShow(true)
  }, [])

  const accept = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('cookie_consent', 'true')
    setShow(false)
  }

  if (!show) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-200 p-4 shadow-[0_-4px_6px_-1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] z-50 animate-slide-in">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center md:text-left">
          🍪 {t('legal_cookie')} 
          <a href="/privacy" className="underline ml-1 text-brand-purple">{t('footer_privacy')}</a>.
        </p>
        <button onClick={accept} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-800 transition whitespace-nowrap">
          {t('legal_accept')}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/ReportModal.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { AlertTriangle, X, Loader2, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function ReportModal({ isOpen, onClose, contextData }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)

  if (!isOpen) return null

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    try {
      await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([{
        name: 'User Report',
        email: 'system@report.com',
        message: `REPORT [${contextData}]: ${message}`
      }])
      setLoading(false)
      setSent(true)
      setTimeout(() => { setSent(false); setMessage(''); onClose() }, 2000)
    } catch (error) {
      setLoading(false)
      alert("Error sending report")
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full relative">
        <button onClick={onClose} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={20}/></button>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 text-orange-600">
          <AlertTriangle />
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{t('report_title')}</h3>
        </div>

        {sent ? (
          <div className="text-center py-8 text-green-600 font-bold">{t('report_sent')}</div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">{t('report_desc')}</p>
            <textarea 
              required
              className="w-full p-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 text-sm mb-4"
              rows="4"
              placeholder={t('report_placeholder')}
              value={message}
              onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}
            ></textarea>
            <button disabled={loading} className="w-full bg-orange-500 text-white font-bold py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-orange-600 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18}/> : <><Send size={18}/> {t('report_btn')}</>}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/Section.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
export default function Section({ children, className = "", id = "" }) {
  return (
    <section id={id} className={`py-20 px-4 md:px-8 ${className}`}>
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">
        {children}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/WebinarForm.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, CheckCircle, Lock } from 'lucide-react'

export default function WebinarForm() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    full_name: "",
    email: "",
    gdpr_consent: false
  })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!formData.gdpr_consent) return alert("You must read the Privacy Policy")
    
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.from('webinar_leads').insert([formData])
    setLoading(false)

    if (error) {
      alert("Registration error: " + error.message)
    } else {
      setSuccess(true)
    }
  }

  if (success) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white p-10 rounded-2xl shadow-xl text-center border-t-4 border-green-500 animate-fade-in">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
          <CheckCircle className="w-10 h-10 text-green-600" />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">You're in! 🎉</h3>
        <p className="text-gray-600">We'll send the Zoom link to your email shortly.</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6 bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md mx-auto border-t-8 border-brand-purple relative z-20">
      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Enter the world of K-Culture!</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Without obstacles. Spots are limited!</p>
      </div>
      
      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">Name</label>
        <input 
          required
          type="text"
          className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none bg-gray-50 focus:bg-white transition"
          value={formData.full_name}
          onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, full_name: e.target.value})}
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-1">Email Address</label>
        <input 
          required
          type="email"
          className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none bg-gray-50 focus:bg-white transition"
          value={formData.email}
          onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <input 
          required
          type="checkbox" 
          id="gdpr"
          className="mt-1 w-4 h-4 text-brand-purple rounded border-gray-300 focus:ring-brand-purple cursor-pointer"
          checked={formData.gdpr_consent}
          onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, gdpr_consent: e.target.checked})}
        />
        <label htmlFor="gdpr" className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed cursor-pointer select-none">
          I have read the <a href="/privacy" className="underline hover:text-brand-purple">Privacy Policy</a>.
        </label>
      </div>

      <button 
        disabled={loading}
        type="submit" 
        className="w-full bg-brand-purple text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl hover:bg-brand-dark disabled:opacity-50 flex justify-center items-center shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition transform active:scale-95"
      >
        {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Send me my ZOOM link!"}
      </button>
      
      <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
        <Lock size={12} /> Your data is safe.
      </p>
    </form>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/AuthContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

const AuthContext = createContext()

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null)
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const router = useRouter()
  
  const supabase = createBrowserClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
  )

  const fetchProfile = async (userId) => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', userId)
        .single()
      
      if (!error) setProfile(data)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Profile fetch error:', error)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const initializeAuth = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
      
      if (session?.user) {
        setUser(session.user)
        await fetchProfile(session.user.id)
      }
      setLoading(false)

      const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (event, session) => {
        if (session?.user) {
          setUser(session.user)
          await fetchProfile(session.user.id)
          router.refresh()
        } else {
          setUser(null)
          setProfile(null)
          router.refresh()
        }
      })

      return () => subscription.unsubscribe()
    }

    initializeAuth()
  }, [])

  const value = {
    signUp: (data) => supabase.auth.signUp(data),
    signIn: (data) => supabase.auth.signInWithPassword(data),
    signOut: async () => {
      await supabase.auth.signOut()
      router.push('/login')
    },
    refreshProfile: () => user && fetchProfile(user.id),
    user,
    profile,
    isAdmin: profile?.role === 'admin',
    loading
  }

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {!loading && children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext)

export function LanguageProvider({ children }) {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState('en')

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('app_lang')
    if (saved) setLang(saved)
  }, [])

  const toggleLanguage = () => {
    const newLang = lang === 'en' ? 'hu' : 'en'
    setLang(newLang)
    localStorage.setItem('app_lang', newLang)
  }

  const t = (key) => {
    return translations[lang][key] || key
  }

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, toggleLanguage, t }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useLanguage = () => useContext(LanguageContext)

PATH: ./src/context/PaymentContext.jsx

import { createContext, useContext, useState } from 'react'
import PaymentModal from '@/components/payment/PaymentModal'
import { useAuth } from './AuthContext'
import { useToast } from './ToastContext'

const PaymentContext = createContext()

export function PaymentProvider({ children }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState(null)

  const initiateCheckout = (item) => {
    if (!user) {
      addToast("Please login to purchase", "error")
      return
    }
    setSelectedItem(item)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  return (
    <PaymentContext.Provider value={{ initiateCheckout }}>
      {children}
      <PaymentModal 
        isOpen={modalOpen} 
        onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)} 
        item={selectedItem} 
      />
    </PaymentContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const usePayment = () => useContext(PaymentContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/ToastContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { X, CheckCircle, AlertCircle, Info } from 'lucide-react'

const ToastContext = createContext()

export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([])

  const addToast = useCallback((message, type = 'info') => {
    const id = Date.now()
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, message, type }])
    setTimeout(() => removeToast(id), 4000)
  }, [])

  const removeToast = useCallback((id) => {
    setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id))
  }, [])

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ addToast }}>
      {children}
      <div className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none">
        {toasts.map(toast => (
          <div 
            key={toast.id}
            className={`pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg border transition-all animate-slide-in min-w-[300px] ${
              toast.type === 'success' ? 'bg-white border-green-100 text-green-800' :
              toast.type === 'error' ? 'bg-white border-red-100 text-red-800' :
              'bg-white border-blue-100 text-brand-dark'
            }`}
          >
            {toast.type === 'success' && <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-500" />}
            {toast.type === 'error' && <AlertCircle size={20} className="text-red-500" />}
            {toast.type === 'info' && <Info size={20} className="text-brand-blue" />}
            
            <p className="text-sm font-bold flex-1">{toast.message}</p>
            
            <button onClick={() => removeToast(toast.id)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useToast = () => useContext(ToastContext)

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'

export function useGamification(userId) {
  // Default mock state
  const [gamification, setGamification] = useState({
    points: 0,
    level: 1,
    streak: 0,
    badges: []
  })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userId) {
        setLoading(false)
        return
    }

    // TODO: Connect to Supabase 'gamification' table here
    // For now, return safe default data so the dashboard loads
    setGamification({
        points: 50,
        level: 1,
        streak: 1,
        badges: []
    })
    setLoading(false)
  }, [userId])

  return { ...gamification, loading }
}

/**
 * Extracts a value from a custom tag in the description.
 * Format: [[TAG_NAME::Value]]
 */
export const extractTag = (text, tagName) => {
  if (!text) return null
  const regex = new RegExp(`\\[\\[${tagName}::(.*?)\\]\\]`)
  const match = text.match(regex)
  return match ? match[1] : null
}

/**
 * Removes all custom tags from the description for display purposes.
 */
export const cleanDescription = (text) => {
  if (!text) return ""
  return text.replace(/\[\[.*?\]\]/g, '').trim()
}

/**
 * Generates a random file path for Supabase storage
 */
export const generateFilePath = (folder, file) => {
  const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()
  const fileName = `${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15)}_${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`
  return `${folder}/${fileName}`
}

import { supabase } from './supabase'

export const initiateCheckout = async (productId, productTitle) => {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return alert("You must log in to purchase.")

  const confirm = window.confirm(`STRIPE SIMULATION:\n\nDo you want to purchase '${productTitle}'?\n(Clicking OK will unlock the content)`)
  
  if (confirm) {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId, status: 'completed' }])
    if (error) {
      console.error(error)
      alert("Error during simulated purchase.")
    } else {
      alert("Purchase successful! The page will refresh.")
      window.location.reload() 
    }
  }
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/lib/video.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const getEmbedUrl = (url) => {
  if (!url) return null;

  // YouTube (Standard)
  if (url.includes('youtube.com/watch?v=')) {
    return url.replace('watch?v=', 'embed/');
  }
  
  // YouTube (Short)
  if (url.includes('youtu.be/')) {
    return url.replace('youtu.be/', 'youtube.com/embed/');
  }
  
  // Vimeo
  if (url.includes('vimeo.com')) {
    const vimeoId = url.split('.com/')[1];
    if (vimeoId && !url.includes('player.vimeo.com')) {
      return `https://player.vimeo.com/video/${vimeoId}`;
    }
  }

  // Google Drive
  if (url.includes('drive.google.com')) {
    return url.replace('/view', '/preview');
  }

  return url;
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/middleware.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export async function middleware(request) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({
    request: {
      headers: request.headers,
    },
  })

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return request.cookies.getAll()
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
          response = NextResponse.next({
            request: {
              headers: request.headers,
            },
          })
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            response.cookies.set(name, value, options)
          )
        },
      },
    }
  )

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()

  // Protected Routes Logic
  const protectedPrefixes = ['/dashboard', '/profile', '/corsi', '/store', '/downloads', '/lezioni-live', '/admin']
  const path = request.nextUrl.pathname
  const isProtected = protectedPrefixes.some(prefix => path.startsWith(prefix))

  // 1. Unauthenticated -> Login
  if (isProtected && !user) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/login', request.url))
  }

  // 2. Authenticated -> Dashboard (if visiting auth pages)
  if (user && (path === '/login' || path === '/register')) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url))
  }

  return response
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp)$).*)',
  ],
}

export const generateInvoice = (user, purchase) => {
  console.log("Generating invoice for:", user.email);
  // Mock implementation for build to pass
  // In a real app, this might use jsPDF
  const invoiceData = {
    id: `INV-${Date.now()}`,
    date: new Date().toISOString(),
    amount: purchase?.price || '0',
    user: user.full_name || user.email
  };
  
  // Return a dummy blob url or success message
  return invoiceData;
}

PATH: ./supabase/functions/send-email/index.ts

// Follow this guide to deploy: https://supabase.com/docs/guides/functions
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts"

const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get('RESEND_API_KEY')

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } })
  }

  try {
    const { record } = await req.json() // The new lead data
    
    // 1. Send Email via Resend (example)
    const res = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        from: 'Annyeong Academy <info@annyeong-academy.com>',
        to: record.email,
        subject: 'Your Korean Open Day Ticket! 🇰🇷',
        html: `
          <h1>Annyeong, ${record.full_name}!</h1>
          <p>You are registered for the webinar on <strong>${record.webinar_date}</strong>.</p>
          <p>Here is your Zoom Link: <a href="https://zoom.us/j/example">Join Here</a></p>
          <p>See you there!</p>
        `
      })
    })

    const data = await res.json()
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  } catch (error) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), { status: 500 })
  }
})

# 🧪 Annyeong Academy - Master Test Plan

**Objective:** Verify that all functional requirements are met and the user experience is smooth before the official launch.
**Environment:** Localhost (`npm run dev`) connected to Supabase.

---

## 🛠️ Phase 0: Prerequisites
Before testing, ensure:
1.  [x] `.env.local` is configured with Supabase URL & Key.
2.  [x] You have run `supabase/MASTER_DB_SETUP.sql` in the Supabase SQL Editor.
3.  [x] Storage buckets (`avatars`, `course-materials`) are created and public.

---

## 1. 📢 Public Funnel (Unauthenticated)
*Goal: Ensure new users can land, read, and register.*

- [x] **Landing Page Load**: Open `http://localhost:5173/`. Ensure no white screen (AdBlocker check).
- [x] **Localization**: Click "HU" in the navbar. Verify text changes to Hungarian. Click "EN" to revert.
- [x] **Webinar Registration**:
    -   Fill out the form at the top.
    -   Leave "GDPR" unchecked -> Verify error message.
    -   Check "GDPR" and submit -> Verify Success Message ("Registration Confirmed").
    -   **Database Check**: Go to Supabase -> `webinar_leads` table. Ensure the row exists.
- [x] **Navigation**: Click links in Navbar (About, Teachers, Contact). Ensure pages load.
- [x] **Contact Form**: Go to `/contact`. Send a message. Verify success UI.

---

## 2. 🔐 Authentication & Security
*Goal: Ensure secure access and role management.*

- [x] **Sign Up**: Go to `/register`. Create a new account (e.g., `student@test.com`).
    -   Verify redirect to Login or Dashboard.
- [x] **Login**: Log out, then log back in.
- [x] **Forgot Password**: Go to `/forgot-password`. Enter email. (Note: Real email sending requires SMTP setup in Supabase, but check if UI feedback works).
- [x] **Protected Routes**:
    -   Log out.
    -   Try to access `http://localhost:5173/dashboard`.
    -   **Expected**: Redirected to `/login`.

---

## 3. 🎓 The Student Experience (LMS)
*Goal: Verify the core value proposition (learning).*

### Dashboard & Catalog

PATH: ./update_project.sh

#!/bin/bash

echo "🔧 Fixing Middleware/Proxy Configuration..."

# 1. Update next.config.js
# We move the rewrites logic here (Single Source of Truth)
# This replaces the logic previously held in vercel.json
cat > next.config.js << 'EOF'
/** @type {import('next').NextConfig} */
const nextConfig = {
  // Allow images from specific domains
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      { protocol: 'https', hostname: 'placehold.co' },
      { protocol: 'https', hostname: 'images.unsplash.com' },
      { protocol: 'https', hostname: 'lkqbkhaklhenhlahgvhj.supabase.co' }
    ],
  },
  // Handle SPA-style fallbacks if necessary (though App Router handles this natively)
  async rewrites() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/:path*',
        destination: '/',
      },
    ]
  },
}

export default nextConfig
EOF

# 2. Remove vercel.json
# Vercel will now use next.config.js automatically.
# Keeping vercel.json with "rewrites" causes the conflict/warning.
if [ -f "vercel.json" ]; then
    echo "🗑️ Removing conflicting vercel.json..."
    rm vercel.json
fi

# 3. Create a clean Vercel config (Optional, only if needed for install command)
# We keep the legacy-peer-deps instruction but remove the routing logic
cat > vercel.json << 'EOF'
{
  "installCommand": "npm install --legacy-peer-deps"
}
EOF

# 4. Clean up any potential duplicate middleware files
# Next.js only allows one middleware file. If you have one in root and one in src, it causes issues.
if [ -f "middleware.js" ] && [ -f "src/middleware.js" ]; then
    echo "⚠️ Found duplicate middleware. Removing root middleware.js (keeping src/middleware.js)..."
    rm middleware.js
fi

# 5. Remove any legacy _middleware files (from Next.js 12 era)
find . -name "_middleware.js" -type f -delete
find . -name "_middleware.jsx" -type f -delete

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "✅ Configuration consolidated."
echo "👉 Run: npm run build"
echo "----------------------------------------------------------------"

================================================================================
PATH: ./vercel.json
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

{
  "installCommand": "npm install --legacy-peer-deps"
}
